Converting scientific notation to decimal\n    2. Replacing commas with periods\n    3. Removing extra whitespace\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value_str) or value_str == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value_str).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period (European decimal format)\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n        value = float(value_str)\n        return value\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef should_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine if a value is likely using an alternative unit based on physiological ranges.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in FEATURE_RANGES:\n        return False\n\n    feature_info = FEATURE_RANGES[feature_name]\n    extended_range = feature_info['extended_range']\n    alt_range = feature_info.get('alt_range')\n\n    # If no alternative range defined, no conversion needed\n    if alt_range is None:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if value is outside extended range (impossible values)\n    if value < 0:\n        return False\n\n    # Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    # Round all numeric columns to 2 decimal places\n    for col in lab_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    print(f\"\\n5. Converting scientific notation to decimal\n    2. Replacing commas with periods\n    3. Removing extra whitespace\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value_str) or value_str == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value_str).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period (European decimal format)\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n        value = float(value_str)\n        return value\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef should_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine if a value is likely using an alternative unit based on physiological ranges.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in FEATURE_RANGES:\n        return False\n\n    feature_info = FEATURE_RANGES[feature_name]\n    extended_range = feature_info['extended_range']\n    alt_range = feature_info.get('alt_range')\n\n    # If no alternative range defined, no conversion needed\n    if alt_range is None:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if value is outside extended range (impossible values)\n    if value < 0:\n        return False\n\n    # If value is within normal extended range, no conversion\n    if extended_range[0] <= value <= extended_range[1]:\n        return False\n\n    # If value is in alternative unit range, consider conversion\n    if alt_range[0] <= value <= alt_range[1]:\n        # After conversion, check if it would fall in acceptable range\n        converted = value * feature_info['alt_unit_factor']\n        if extended_range[0] <= converted <= extended_range[1]:\n            return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert value from alternative unit to US conventional unit.\n    \"\"\"\n    if feature_name not in FEATURE_RANGES:\n        return value\n\n    feature_info = FEATURE_RANGES[feature_name]\n    return value * feature_info['alt_unit_factor']\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    initial_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Initial rows: {initial_rows}\")\n\n    # Identifying and converting values with alternative units...\")\n    conversion_stats = {}\n\n    for col in lab_columns:\n        conversions = 0\n        for idx in df_clean.index:\n            value = df_clean.at[idx, col]\n            if should_convert_unit(value, col):\n                df_clean.at[idx, col] = convert_unit(value, col)\n                conversions += 1\n\n        if conversions > 0:\n            conversion_stats[col] = conversions\n            print(f\"  {col}: {conversions} values converted\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal features with conversions: {len(conversion_stats)}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n4. Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    # Round all numeric columns to 2 decimal places\n    for col in lab_columns:\n        df_clean[col] = df_clean[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else x)\n\n    print(f\"\\n5. Saving harmonized data to {output_file}...\")\n    df_clean.to_csv(output_file, index=False)\n\n    final_rows = len(df_clean)\n    print(f\"\\nHarmonization complete!\")\n    print(f\"Final rows: {final_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Total columns: {len(df_clean.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Data saved to: {output_file}\")\n\n    return df_clean\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    input_file = \"/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv\"\n    output_file = \"/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv\"\n\n    harmonized_data = harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Converting scientific notation to decimal\n    2. Replacing commas with periods\n    3. Removing extra whitespace\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value_str) or value_str == '':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Convert to string and strip whitespace\n    value_str = str(value_str).strip()\n\n    # Replace comma with period (European decimal format)\n    value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n\n    try:\n        # Convert to float (handles scientific notation automatically)\n        value = float(value_str)\n        return value\n    except ValueError:\n        return np.nan\n\n\ndef should_convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Determine if a value is likely using an alternative unit based on physiological ranges.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value) or feature_name not in FEATURE_RANGES:\n        return False\n\n    feature_info = FEATURE_RANGES[feature_name]\n    extended_range = feature_info['extended_range']\n    alt_range = feature_info.get('alt_range')\n\n    # If no alternative range defined, no conversion needed\n    if alt_range is None:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if value is outside extended range (impossible values)\n    if value < 0:\n        return False\n\n    # If value is within normal extended range, no conversion\n    if extended_range[0] <= value <= extended_range[1]:\n        return False\n\n    # If value is in alternative unit range, consider conversion\n    if alt_range[0] <= value <= alt_range[1]:\n        # After conversion, check if it would fall in acceptable range\n        converted = value * feature_info['alt_unit_factor']\n        if extended_range[0] <= converted <= extended_range[1]:\n            return True\n\n    return False\n\n\ndef convert_unit(value, feature_name):\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert value from alternative unit to US conventional unit.\n    \"\"\"\n    if feature_name not in FEATURE_RANGES:\n        return value\n\n    feature_info = FEATURE_RANGES[feature_name]\n    return value * feature_info['alt_unit_factor']\n\n\ndef harmonize_lab_data(input_file, output_file):\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize clinical lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(f\"Reading data from {input_file}...\")\n    df = pd.read_csv(input_file)\n\n    initial_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Initial rows: {initial_rows}\")\n\n    # Get all column names except patient_id\n    lab_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\n1. Cleaning numeric values (scientific notation, decimal format)...\")\n    # Clean all numeric columns\n    for col in lab_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(clean_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"\\n2.
